Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the genomics structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 611. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 985 and converges at rate O(1/n^4).
